2193.0s 3420 Baseline mAP (0 synthetic samples): 0.6132

2193.0s 3422 Best mAP among augmented runs was 0.6132 with 0 samples (compare to baseline 0.6132).
2193.0s 3423 
2193.0s 3424 Possible reasons for lack of improvement or worsening:
2193.0s 3425 1. Poor GAN Quality: The DCGAN might have generated unrealistic or low-quality images (mode collapse, artifacts) that confused the classifier or didn't represent the true data distribution.
2193.0s 3426 2. Distribution Shift: The synthetic data distribution might be significantly different from the real data distribution, leading the classifier astray.
2193.0s 3427 3. Simplistic Labeling: Assigning only a single label to synthetic images might contradict the multi-label nature of the real data, potentially harming performance on complex images.
2193.0s 3428 4. Sufficient Original Data: The original training set might already be large or diverse enough for the chosen classifier architecture, making augmentation less impactful or even detrimental if the synthetic data is noisy.
2193.0s 3429 5. Suboptimal Augmentation Amount: The tested amounts (100, 200, 500) might not be optimal. Too few samples might not help, while too many poor-quality samples could hurt.
